# Board

Written only by the Product Owner and the Release Captain. One row per
slice. See `docs/v10/SWARM.md`.

## Status format (normalized for `scripts/v10-pulse.sh`)

Every row's `Status` cell is exactly one token, an `@`, and a UTC timestamp
(`<token>@YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MMZ`) -- the time the status last changed. Prose,
history, and caveats move to `Notes`; `Status` itself must stay
machine-parseable with no free text. Tokens, in the lifecycle order the pulse
script expects:

`ready` -> `building` -> `review` -> `review-blocked` (returns to `building`)
-> `approved` -> `merged` -> `released`. Side states: `blocked` (needs a
Captain/PO decision), `rejected` (sent back, will be re-cut), `parked`
(deliberately shelved).

`merged` here means "the Captain landed it on main" -- a slice sits at
`merged` until the Captain flips it to `released` on the next version bump
that ships it. The pulse script does NOT trust this field for its own
"merged-but-unreleased age" computation: it derives that directly from git
(`git log` on main after the last release tag) since a table cell can go
stale between edits. This field still matters for humans and for the
ready/building/review/blocked counts, which git cannot derive.

| S-18 | BACKLOG 149 (escalated from BACKLOG 44, real production gap not just test-coverage): the GH CLI's own `hosts.yml` credential file is completely unprotected by Rule-of-Two's env-var withhold -- an injected prompt can just run real `gh`, which resolves credentials from `$HOME` regardless of the withheld env vars | autonomy/run.sh (the withhold call site), loki-ts/src/runner/github_token.ts (Bun equivalent), tests/moat/p9-rule-of-two.sh | HIGH | released@2026-09-28T01:11Z | **ROUND 2 REJECT (new reviewer), a different and more severe bypass than round 1**: round 1's fix (env sentinel + GH_CONFIG_DIR + git credential.helper reset via GIT_CONFIG_COUNT/KEY/VALUE) correctly closes both round-1-confirmed bypasses -- verified independently by BOTH round-2 reviewers (one APPROVE, one REJECT, but the REJECT's own verification of the round-1 fixes is itself a clean pass). The REJECT is for a THIRD, previously undisclosed and unhandled vector: **SSH transport is completely unhandled**. Every round-1 mechanism (token sentinel, GH_CONFIG_DIR, credential.helper reset) covers only HTTPS-based git/gh credential resolution -- none touch SSH_AUTH_SOCK, GIT_SSH_COMMAND, core.sshCommand, or ~/.ssh. Reproduced live, read-only: `ssh-add -l` exits 0 (the provider session's live, untouched $HOME/env always has a reachable SSH agent by the fix's own design), and `ssh -T git@github.com` completes full auth as the real account, completely unaffected by every round-1 mitigation. An agent can simply `git remote set-url origin git@github.com:<owner>/<repo>.git && git push` and authenticate exactly as an unrestricted session -- simpler than the already-disclosed `gh auth token -u <username>` gap (no username lookup, no keychain tooling, just the operator's normal always-on ssh-agent). The P9 test's own PASS assertion text is unqualified and false for this case. Fix path per the reviewer: extend the existing capture/apply/restore/depth-counter architecture (already correct for the HTTPS vars) to also sentinel SSH_AUTH_SOCK and override GIT_SSH_COMMAND/core.sshCommand for the withheld session -- a bounded addition to an already-sound design, not a redesign. Two secondary non-blocking findings folded into the rework: (1) `env -u GH_TOKEN -u GITHUB_TOKEN gh auth token` is an undisclosed sibling of the already-disclosed username-lookup gap, needs naming; (2) the Bun/TS route has no git-version floor check the bash route already added, silently no-oping the credential.helper reset on git < 2.31 with no operator signal. Per the founder's D25 directive, this does NOT block the current release train -- the vulnerability this slice fixes (both round-1 and this newly-found SSH gap) is already live in published v9.55.0 either way; S-18 ships in its own train the moment its review reaches unanimous APPROVE. Every slice above: implement with red-then-green tests, run diff-scoped tests
plus `bash tests/moat/run.sh` in your own worktree, never touch `main`,
`VERSION`, or a hot file/bundle (see below), and return BLOCKED (never guess
past your declared file set) if you need a file outside it. `git merge
--ff-only <captain's current main sha>` first; worktrees are cut from
origin/main. No version bumps, no emojis, no em or en dashes, bash 3.2
compatible, shellcheck clean at CI flags on changed .sh files. Stage by
name, conventional commit message, no Co-Authored-By, end with the line:
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_019U6NAdpZyYeCEKzvHfZ4Tq. Do
not push.
